# Azure Resource Graph queries

The workbook runs **33 read-only Azure Resource Graph queries**. Nothing is written, no agent is required, and no data leaves the tenant. Non-quota queries use the `Subscriptions` picker. Quota queries can read all readable subscriptions in the current tenant and apply their separate planning-scope dropdown; the headroom view uses tenant-wide candidates.

Assess > V5 / V6 paths also has one **read-only ARM GETARRAY** inspector for `/subscriptions/{LiveSubscription}/providers/Microsoft.Compute/skus`, API version `2021-07-01`, filtered to `{LiveRegion}`. Its JSONPath selects the exact `{LiveTarget}` and preserves full restrictions, location/zone information and capabilities. The target is exported from a selected placement combination. A hidden native Merge parameter reuses that record without another API request or customer click. The `eligibility-status` ARG query evaluates the record and renders red Restricted, green Not restricted or amber Unknown. Raw evidence is opt-in. A green access result is not quota, policy or physical allocation approval.

Quota checks use indexed Compute `QuotaResources` rather than per-row ARM lookups. This enables automatic cross-subscription checks but is eventually consistent; verify with Azure Quotas/CLI immediately before migration.

This file is generated by `scripts/build_queries_doc.py` - edit the build script, not this file.

## A note on the embedded lookup

Azure Resource Graph supports neither `let` nor `datatable`, so the 628-size lifecycle catalogue is stored once in hidden text parameters. Workbooks substitutes the parameters into each query before sending it to ARG, producing a `parse_json('{...}')` dynamic literal indexed by VM size. That literal is about 84 KB and is elided throughout this document for readability. It is generated from `references/sku-migration-map.json`; warning codes are stored as single characters and region lists as hex indices into a shared region table. Sharing the lookups keeps the saved workbook below the 1 MB limit without removing any sizes or queries.

The fields packed into each lookup record, in order:

| # | Field | Meaning |
| ---: | --- | --- |
| 0 | Lifecycle | `RETIRE`, `EOL` or `OPTIONAL` |
| 1 | SeriesGroup | Announcement grouping, used for the source link |
| 2 | RetirementDate | Announced retirement date, empty when none |
| 3 | RecommendedTarget | Default same-or-better replacement size |
| 4 | MemoryPreservingTarget | Alternative when the default reduces RAM |
| 5-6 | TargetVcpu, TargetMemoryGB | Target spec |
| 7-8 | CurrentVcpu, CurrentMemoryGB | Source spec |
| 9 | WarnCodes | Single-character prerequisite codes |
| 10 | AlternateTargets | Up to three alternates |
| 11 | RiEnded | Reserved Instance purchase and renewal end date |
| 12 | Series | Size series name |
| 13 | PriceUpdate | `1` when the series is in scope for 1 Feb 2027 |
| 14-15 | MpVcpu, MpMemoryGB | Memory-preserving target spec |

## The shared base query

Every fleet query below starts with this pipeline. It inventories the VMs and scale sets, joins each to its managed OS disk to read the real `hyperVGeneration`, looks the size up in the lifecycle catalogue, and computes a platform-only score (legacy field `ReadinessScore`), not application approval. Per-query tails are shown in the sections that follow and should be appended to this prefix.

```kusto

```

## Inventory - Fleet overview

### `ov-tiles`

Fleet summary tiles: impacted resources, instances, retirement vs end-of-life split, count below readiness 60, fleet readiness score and days to the next retirement.

- **Visualization:** tiles
- **Scope:** `{Subscriptions}`
- **Base:** appended to the shared base query above

```kusto
resources
| where type =~ 'microsoft.compute/virtualmachines' or type =~ 'microsoft.compute/virtualmachinescalesets'
| extend IsVmss = (type =~ 'microsoft.compute/virtualmachinescalesets')
// A Flexible-orchestration scale set surfaces BOTH the scale set (carrying
// sku.capacity) and every member VM as separate Resource Graph rows. Counting
// both double-reports the fleet, so member VMs are dropped here and the scale
// set alone represents them. Uniform scale sets do not expose member VMs at
// all, so they are unaffected. Flexible is the current Azure default, so this
// matters for most modern fleets.

| where IsVmss or isempty(tostring(properties.virtualMachineScaleSet.id))
| extend VmSize = iff(IsVmss, tostring(sku.name), tostring(properties.hardwareProfile.vmSize))
| extend Instances = iff(IsVmss, toint(sku.capacity), 1)
| extend OsType = iff(IsVmss, tostring(properties.virtualMachineProfile.storageProfile.osDisk.osType), tostring(properties.storageProfile.osDisk.osType))
| extend OsDiskId = tolower(tostring(properties.storageProfile.osDisk.managedDisk.id))
| extend AvSetId = tostring(properties.availabilitySet.id)
| extend PowerState = tostring(split(tostring(properties.extended.instanceView.powerState.code), '/')[1])
| extend ComputePriority = iff(IsVmss, tostring(properties.virtualMachineProfile.priority), tostring(properties.priority))
| extend Zone = tostring(zones[0])
| extend ImageSku = iff(IsVmss, tostring(properties.virtualMachineProfile.storageProfile.imageReference.sku), tostring(properties.storageProfile.imageReference.sku))
| extend ImageId = iff(IsVmss, tostring(coalesce(properties.virtualMachineProfile.storageProfile.imageReference.id, properties.virtualMachineProfile.storageProfile.imageReference.sharedGalleryImageId, properties.virtualMachineProfile.storageProfile.imageReference.communityGalleryImageId)), tostring(coalesce(properties.storageProfile.imageReference.id, properties.storageProfile.imageReference.sharedGalleryImageId, properties.storageProfile.imageReference.communityGalleryImageId)))
| extend ImagePublisher = iff(IsVmss, tostring(properties.virtualMachineProfile.storageProfile.imageReference.publisher), tostring(properties.storageProfile.imageReference.publisher))
| extend ImageOffer = iff(IsVmss, tostring(properties.virtualMachineProfile.storageProfile.imageReference.offer), tostring(properties.storageProfile.imageReference.offer))
| extend GuestOsName = tostring(properties.extended.instanceView.osName)
| project ResourceId = id, Name = name, ResourceGroup = resourceGroup, SubscriptionId = subscriptionId,
          Region = location, VmSize, Instances, OsType, OsDiskId, AvSetId, PowerState, ComputePriority, Zone, ImageSku, ImageId, ImagePublisher, ImageOffer, GuestOsName, IsVmss, Tags = tags
| join kind=leftouter (
    resources
    | where type =~ 'microsoft.compute/disks'
    | project OsDiskId = tolower(id), DiskGen = tostring(properties.hyperVGeneration),
              DiskAdeConfigured = tobool(properties.encryptionSettingsCollection.enabled),
              DiskControllers = tostring(properties.supportedCapabilities.diskControllerTypes)
  ) on OsDiskId
| extend VmGeneration = case(
        isnotempty(DiskGen), DiskGen,
        ImageSku has 'gen2' or ImageSku has '-g2', 'V2',
        'Unknown')
| extend Rec = tostring(parse_json('{ ...lifecycle lookup, see note below... }')[VmSize])
| where isnotempty(Rec)
| extend P = split(Rec, '|')
| extend Lifecycle = tostring(P[0]),
         SeriesGroup = tostring(P[1]),
         RetirementDate = tostring(P[2]),
         RecommendedTarget = tostring(P[3]),
         MemoryPreservingTarget = tostring(P[4]),
         TargetVcpu = tostring(P[5]),
         TargetMemoryGB = tostring(P[6]),
         CurrentVcpu = toint(P[7]),
         CurrentMemoryGB = toreal(P[8]),
         WarnCodes = tostring(P[9]),
         AlternateTargets = tostring(P[10]),
         RiEnded = tostring(P[11]),
         Series = tostring(P[12]),
         PriceUpdate = tostring(P[13]),
         MpVcpu = tostring(P[14]),
         MpMemoryGB = tostring(P[15])
| extend RetiresOn = iff(isempty(RetirementDate), datetime(null), todatetime(RetirementDate))
| extend DaysToRetirement = iff(isempty(RetirementDate), toint(-1), datetime_diff('day', todatetime(RetirementDate), now()))
| extend RegionId = tostring(parse_json('{ ...lifecycle lookup, see note below... }')[tolower(Region)])
| extend TargetRegions = tostring(parse_json('{ ...lifecycle lookup, see note below... }')[RecommendedTarget])
| extend TargetInRegion = case(
        isempty(RecommendedTarget) or isempty(TargetRegions), 'Unverified',
        isempty(RegionId), 'Unverified',
        TargetRegions has strcat('|', RegionId, '|'), 'Yes',
        'No')
| extend PriceUpdate2027 = iff(PriceUpdate == '1', 'Yes', 'No')
// Reserved Instance purchases and renewals for these series ended 2026-07-01.
// This series-wide cutoff is not a customer's reservation expiry or billing coverage.
| extend RiEndsOn = iff(isempty(RiEnded), datetime(null), todatetime(RiEnded))
| extend RiStatus = case(
        isempty(RiEnded), 'No cutoff in captured catalogue; coverage not assessed',
        todatetime(RiEnded) < now(), 'Series purchase/renewal cutoff passed; coverage not assessed',
        'Series purchase/renewal cutoff upcoming; coverage not assessed')
// ---- readiness penalties, evaluated against the REAL fleet attributes ----
| extend NeedsGen2 = iff(WarnCodes has 'g' and VmGeneration == 'V1', 1, 0)
| extend GenUnknown = iff(WarnCodes has 'g' and VmGeneration == 'Unknown', 1, 0)
| extend PenGen = iff(NeedsGen2 == 1, 45, iff(GenUnknown == 1, 20, 0))
| extend PenArch = iff(WarnCodes has 'a', 50, 0)
| extend PenMem = iff(WarnCodes has 'm', 25, 0)
| extend PenTemp = iff(WarnCodes has 't', 20, 0)
| extend PenNvme = iff(WarnCodes has 'n', 15, 0)
| extend PenNarrow = iff(WarnCodes has 'r', 10, 0)
| extend PenAz = iff(WarnCodes has 'z' and isnotempty(Zone), 10, 0)
| extend PenVcpu = iff(WarnCodes has 'v', 5, 0)
| extend PenRegion = iff(TargetInRegion == 'No', 30, 0)
| extend PenAvSet = iff(isnotempty(AvSetId), 5, 0)
| extend PenVmss = iff(IsVmss, 5, 0)
| extend ReadinessScore = toint(iff(100 - PenGen - PenArch - PenMem - PenTemp - PenNvme - PenNarrow - PenAz - PenVcpu - PenRegion - PenAvSet - PenVmss < 0, 0, 100 - PenGen - PenArch - PenMem - PenTemp - PenNvme - PenNarrow - PenAz - PenVcpu - PenRegion - PenAvSet - PenVmss))
| extend Complexity = case(
        ReadinessScore >= 85, 'Few scored platform changes - validation required',
        ReadinessScore >= 60, 'Moderate scored platform changes',
        ReadinessScore >= 35, 'Substantial scored platform changes',
        'Major scored platform changes')
| extend MigrationApproval = 'Not assessed - guest, application, HA, backup and rollback evidence required'
| extend Urgency = case(
        DaysToRetirement < 0, 'No retirement date',
        DaysToRetirement <= 180, 'Critical - under 6 months',
        DaysToRetirement <= 365, 'High - under 12 months',
        DaysToRetirement <= 730, 'Medium - under 24 months',
        'Planned - over 24 months')
| extend ActionCategory = case(
        Lifecycle == 'RETIRE', 'Retirement announced - action required',
        Lifecycle == 'EOL', 'End of life - plan migration',
        'Optional modernization')
| extend OfficialLifecycleStage = case(
        Lifecycle == 'EOL', 'End of life - captured announcement',
        'Unknown - reconcile official Current/Extended/End-of-life/Retired stage; announcement alone is not stage proof')
| extend Blockers = trim(', ', strcat(
        iff(NeedsGen2 == 1, 'Gen1 to Gen2 conversion, ', ''),
        iff(GenUnknown == 1, 'Confirm VM generation, ', ''),
        iff(PenArch > 0, 'Arm64 port, ', ''),
        iff(PenMem > 0, 'Memory downgrade, ', ''),
        iff(PenTemp > 0, 'Temp disk dependency, ', ''),
        iff(PenNvme > 0, 'NVMe boot validation, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'h', 'MANA/accelerated networking validation, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'u', 'No suitable v5 target - manual review, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'k', 'No suitable target in this path - manual selection, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'l', 'Target local-disk capacity unverified, ', ''),
        iff(PenRegion > 0, 'Target not in region, ', ''),
        iff(PenVcpu > 0, 'vCPU count change, ', '')))
| extend Blockers = iff(isempty(Blockers), 'None identified', Blockers)
// Size-level considerations, independent of any individual VM's configuration.
// Used by the size-by-size mapping so rows do not split on per-VM attributes.
| extend SizeConsiderations = trim(', ', strcat(
        iff(WarnCodes has 'g', 'Target is Gen2-only, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'a', 'Arm64 architecture change, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'm', 'Memory reduction at same vCPU, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 't', 'Local temp disk removed, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'n', 'Target requires NVMe boot validation, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'h', 'Hardware-dependent MANA validation, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'u', 'No suitable v5 target - manual review, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'k', 'No suitable target in this path - manual selection, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'l', 'Target local-disk capacity unverified, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'r', 'Narrow target region footprint, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'z', 'Limited zone coverage, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'v', 'No target at current vCPU count, ', '')))
| extend SizeConsiderations = iff(isempty(SizeConsiderations), 'Straight resize', SizeConsiderations)
| summarize Impacted = countif(Lifecycle != 'OPTIONAL'),
            Instances = sumif(Instances, Lifecycle != 'OPTIONAL'),
            Retiring = countif(Lifecycle == 'RETIRE'),
            EndOfLife = countif(Lifecycle == 'EOL'),
            Blocked = countif(Lifecycle != 'OPTIONAL' and ReadinessScore < 60),
            ScoreSum = sumif(ReadinessScore, Lifecycle != 'OPTIONAL'),
            SoonestDays = min(iff(DaysToRetirement >= 0, DaysToRetirement, 99999))
| extend Score = iff(Impacted == 0, 100, toint(round(todouble(ScoreSum) / todouble(Impacted), 0)))
| extend SoonestDays = iff(SoonestDays == 99999, -1, SoonestDays)
| extend M = pack_array('Impacted resources', 'Instances affected', 'Retirement announced',
                        'End of life', 'Need prerequisite work', 'Fleet platform score',
                        'Days to next retirement')
| mv-expand Metric = M to typeof(string)
| extend Value = case(
        Metric == 'Impacted resources', Impacted,
        Metric == 'Instances affected', Instances,
        Metric == 'Retirement announced', Retiring,
        Metric == 'End of life', EndOfLife,
        Metric == 'Need prerequisite work', Blocked,
        Metric == 'Fleet platform score', Score,
        SoonestDays)
| extend Detail = case(
        Metric == 'Fleet platform score', case(Score >= 85, 'Few scored differences; validation required',
                                                Score >= 60, 'Validation needed',
                                                Score >= 35, 'Prerequisite work required',
                                                'Significant re-architecture'),
        Metric == 'Days to next retirement', iff(Value < 0, 'No dated retirements', 'days remaining'),
        Metric == 'Need prerequisite work', 'platform score below 60',
        'resources')
| extend Ord = case(
        Metric == 'Impacted resources', 1,
        Metric == 'Instances affected', 2,
        Metric == 'Retirement announced', 3,
        Metric == 'End of life', 4,
        Metric == 'Need prerequisite work', 5,
        Metric == 'Fleet platform score', 6,
        7)
| order by Ord asc
| project Metric, Value, Detail
```

### `ov-urgency`

Impacted instances grouped by urgency band.

- **Visualization:** piechart
- **Scope:** `{Subscriptions}`
- **Base:** appended to the shared base query above

```kusto
resources
| where type =~ 'microsoft.compute/virtualmachines' or type =~ 'microsoft.compute/virtualmachinescalesets'
| extend IsVmss = (type =~ 'microsoft.compute/virtualmachinescalesets')
// A Flexible-orchestration scale set surfaces BOTH the scale set (carrying
// sku.capacity) and every member VM as separate Resource Graph rows. Counting
// both double-reports the fleet, so member VMs are dropped here and the scale
// set alone represents them. Uniform scale sets do not expose member VMs at
// all, so they are unaffected. Flexible is the current Azure default, so this
// matters for most modern fleets.

| where IsVmss or isempty(tostring(properties.virtualMachineScaleSet.id))
| extend VmSize = iff(IsVmss, tostring(sku.name), tostring(properties.hardwareProfile.vmSize))
| extend Instances = iff(IsVmss, toint(sku.capacity), 1)
| extend OsType = iff(IsVmss, tostring(properties.virtualMachineProfile.storageProfile.osDisk.osType), tostring(properties.storageProfile.osDisk.osType))
| extend OsDiskId = tolower(tostring(properties.storageProfile.osDisk.managedDisk.id))
| extend AvSetId = tostring(properties.availabilitySet.id)
| extend PowerState = tostring(split(tostring(properties.extended.instanceView.powerState.code), '/')[1])
| extend ComputePriority = iff(IsVmss, tostring(properties.virtualMachineProfile.priority), tostring(properties.priority))
| extend Zone = tostring(zones[0])
| extend ImageSku = iff(IsVmss, tostring(properties.virtualMachineProfile.storageProfile.imageReference.sku), tostring(properties.storageProfile.imageReference.sku))
| extend ImageId = iff(IsVmss, tostring(coalesce(properties.virtualMachineProfile.storageProfile.imageReference.id, properties.virtualMachineProfile.storageProfile.imageReference.sharedGalleryImageId, properties.virtualMachineProfile.storageProfile.imageReference.communityGalleryImageId)), tostring(coalesce(properties.storageProfile.imageReference.id, properties.storageProfile.imageReference.sharedGalleryImageId, properties.storageProfile.imageReference.communityGalleryImageId)))
| extend ImagePublisher = iff(IsVmss, tostring(properties.virtualMachineProfile.storageProfile.imageReference.publisher), tostring(properties.storageProfile.imageReference.publisher))
| extend ImageOffer = iff(IsVmss, tostring(properties.virtualMachineProfile.storageProfile.imageReference.offer), tostring(properties.storageProfile.imageReference.offer))
| extend GuestOsName = tostring(properties.extended.instanceView.osName)
| project ResourceId = id, Name = name, ResourceGroup = resourceGroup, SubscriptionId = subscriptionId,
          Region = location, VmSize, Instances, OsType, OsDiskId, AvSetId, PowerState, ComputePriority, Zone, ImageSku, ImageId, ImagePublisher, ImageOffer, GuestOsName, IsVmss, Tags = tags
| join kind=leftouter (
    resources
    | where type =~ 'microsoft.compute/disks'
    | project OsDiskId = tolower(id), DiskGen = tostring(properties.hyperVGeneration),
              DiskAdeConfigured = tobool(properties.encryptionSettingsCollection.enabled),
              DiskControllers = tostring(properties.supportedCapabilities.diskControllerTypes)
  ) on OsDiskId
| extend VmGeneration = case(
        isnotempty(DiskGen), DiskGen,
        ImageSku has 'gen2' or ImageSku has '-g2', 'V2',
        'Unknown')
| extend Rec = tostring(parse_json('{ ...lifecycle lookup, see note below... }')[VmSize])
| where isnotempty(Rec)
| extend P = split(Rec, '|')
| extend Lifecycle = tostring(P[0]),
         SeriesGroup = tostring(P[1]),
         RetirementDate = tostring(P[2]),
         RecommendedTarget = tostring(P[3]),
         MemoryPreservingTarget = tostring(P[4]),
         TargetVcpu = tostring(P[5]),
         TargetMemoryGB = tostring(P[6]),
         CurrentVcpu = toint(P[7]),
         CurrentMemoryGB = toreal(P[8]),
         WarnCodes = tostring(P[9]),
         AlternateTargets = tostring(P[10]),
         RiEnded = tostring(P[11]),
         Series = tostring(P[12]),
         PriceUpdate = tostring(P[13]),
         MpVcpu = tostring(P[14]),
         MpMemoryGB = tostring(P[15])
| extend RetiresOn = iff(isempty(RetirementDate), datetime(null), todatetime(RetirementDate))
| extend DaysToRetirement = iff(isempty(RetirementDate), toint(-1), datetime_diff('day', todatetime(RetirementDate), now()))
| extend RegionId = tostring(parse_json('{ ...lifecycle lookup, see note below... }')[tolower(Region)])
| extend TargetRegions = tostring(parse_json('{ ...lifecycle lookup, see note below... }')[RecommendedTarget])
| extend TargetInRegion = case(
        isempty(RecommendedTarget) or isempty(TargetRegions), 'Unverified',
        isempty(RegionId), 'Unverified',
        TargetRegions has strcat('|', RegionId, '|'), 'Yes',
        'No')
| extend PriceUpdate2027 = iff(PriceUpdate == '1', 'Yes', 'No')
// Reserved Instance purchases and renewals for these series ended 2026-07-01.
// This series-wide cutoff is not a customer's reservation expiry or billing coverage.
| extend RiEndsOn = iff(isempty(RiEnded), datetime(null), todatetime(RiEnded))
| extend RiStatus = case(
        isempty(RiEnded), 'No cutoff in captured catalogue; coverage not assessed',
        todatetime(RiEnded) < now(), 'Series purchase/renewal cutoff passed; coverage not assessed',
        'Series purchase/renewal cutoff upcoming; coverage not assessed')
// ---- readiness penalties, evaluated against the REAL fleet attributes ----
| extend NeedsGen2 = iff(WarnCodes has 'g' and VmGeneration == 'V1', 1, 0)
| extend GenUnknown = iff(WarnCodes has 'g' and VmGeneration == 'Unknown', 1, 0)
| extend PenGen = iff(NeedsGen2 == 1, 45, iff(GenUnknown == 1, 20, 0))
| extend PenArch = iff(WarnCodes has 'a', 50, 0)
| extend PenMem = iff(WarnCodes has 'm', 25, 0)
| extend PenTemp = iff(WarnCodes has 't', 20, 0)
| extend PenNvme = iff(WarnCodes has 'n', 15, 0)
| extend PenNarrow = iff(WarnCodes has 'r', 10, 0)
| extend PenAz = iff(WarnCodes has 'z' and isnotempty(Zone), 10, 0)
| extend PenVcpu = iff(WarnCodes has 'v', 5, 0)
| extend PenRegion = iff(TargetInRegion == 'No', 30, 0)
| extend PenAvSet = iff(isnotempty(AvSetId), 5, 0)
| extend PenVmss = iff(IsVmss, 5, 0)
| extend ReadinessScore = toint(iff(100 - PenGen - PenArch - PenMem - PenTemp - PenNvme - PenNarrow - PenAz - PenVcpu - PenRegion - PenAvSet - PenVmss < 0, 0, 100 - PenGen - PenArch - PenMem - PenTemp - PenNvme - PenNarrow - PenAz - PenVcpu - PenRegion - PenAvSet - PenVmss))
| extend Complexity = case(
        ReadinessScore >= 85, 'Few scored platform changes - validation required',
        ReadinessScore >= 60, 'Moderate scored platform changes',
        ReadinessScore >= 35, 'Substantial scored platform changes',
        'Major scored platform changes')
| extend MigrationApproval = 'Not assessed - guest, application, HA, backup and rollback evidence required'
| extend Urgency = case(
        DaysToRetirement < 0, 'No retirement date',
        DaysToRetirement <= 180, 'Critical - under 6 months',
        DaysToRetirement <= 365, 'High - under 12 months',
        DaysToRetirement <= 730, 'Medium - under 24 months',
        'Planned - over 24 months')
| extend ActionCategory = case(
        Lifecycle == 'RETIRE', 'Retirement announced - action required',
        Lifecycle == 'EOL', 'End of life - plan migration',
        'Optional modernization')
| extend OfficialLifecycleStage = case(
        Lifecycle == 'EOL', 'End of life - captured announcement',
        'Unknown - reconcile official Current/Extended/End-of-life/Retired stage; announcement alone is not stage proof')
| extend Blockers = trim(', ', strcat(
        iff(NeedsGen2 == 1, 'Gen1 to Gen2 conversion, ', ''),
        iff(GenUnknown == 1, 'Confirm VM generation, ', ''),
        iff(PenArch > 0, 'Arm64 port, ', ''),
        iff(PenMem > 0, 'Memory downgrade, ', ''),
        iff(PenTemp > 0, 'Temp disk dependency, ', ''),
        iff(PenNvme > 0, 'NVMe boot validation, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'h', 'MANA/accelerated networking validation, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'u', 'No suitable v5 target - manual review, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'k', 'No suitable target in this path - manual selection, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'l', 'Target local-disk capacity unverified, ', ''),
        iff(PenRegion > 0, 'Target not in region, ', ''),
        iff(PenVcpu > 0, 'vCPU count change, ', '')))
| extend Blockers = iff(isempty(Blockers), 'None identified', Blockers)
// Size-level considerations, independent of any individual VM's configuration.
// Used by the size-by-size mapping so rows do not split on per-VM attributes.
| extend SizeConsiderations = trim(', ', strcat(
        iff(WarnCodes has 'g', 'Target is Gen2-only, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'a', 'Arm64 architecture change, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'm', 'Memory reduction at same vCPU, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 't', 'Local temp disk removed, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'n', 'Target requires NVMe boot validation, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'h', 'Hardware-dependent MANA validation, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'u', 'No suitable v5 target - manual review, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'k', 'No suitable target in this path - manual selection, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'l', 'Target local-disk capacity unverified, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'r', 'Narrow target region footprint, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'z', 'Limited zone coverage, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'v', 'No target at current vCPU count, ', '')))
| extend SizeConsiderations = iff(isempty(SizeConsiderations), 'Straight resize', SizeConsiderations)
| where Lifecycle != 'OPTIONAL'
| summarize Instances = sum(Instances) by Urgency
| order by Instances desc
```

### `ov-gen`

Impacted resources by the actual Hyper-V generation read from each managed OS disk.

- **Visualization:** piechart
- **Scope:** `{Subscriptions}`
- **Base:** appended to the shared base query above

```kusto
resources
| where type =~ 'microsoft.compute/virtualmachines' or type =~ 'microsoft.compute/virtualmachinescalesets'
| extend IsVmss = (type =~ 'microsoft.compute/virtualmachinescalesets')
// A Flexible-orchestration scale set surfaces BOTH the scale set (carrying
// sku.capacity) and every member VM as separate Resource Graph rows. Counting
// both double-reports the fleet, so member VMs are dropped here and the scale
// set alone represents them. Uniform scale sets do not expose member VMs at
// all, so they are unaffected. Flexible is the current Azure default, so this
// matters for most modern fleets.

| where IsVmss or isempty(tostring(properties.virtualMachineScaleSet.id))
| extend VmSize = iff(IsVmss, tostring(sku.name), tostring(properties.hardwareProfile.vmSize))
| extend Instances = iff(IsVmss, toint(sku.capacity), 1)
| extend OsType = iff(IsVmss, tostring(properties.virtualMachineProfile.storageProfile.osDisk.osType), tostring(properties.storageProfile.osDisk.osType))
| extend OsDiskId = tolower(tostring(properties.storageProfile.osDisk.managedDisk.id))
| extend AvSetId = tostring(properties.availabilitySet.id)
| extend PowerState = tostring(split(tostring(properties.extended.instanceView.powerState.code), '/')[1])
| extend ComputePriority = iff(IsVmss, tostring(properties.virtualMachineProfile.priority), tostring(properties.priority))
| extend Zone = tostring(zones[0])
| extend ImageSku = iff(IsVmss, tostring(properties.virtualMachineProfile.storageProfile.imageReference.sku), tostring(properties.storageProfile.imageReference.sku))
| extend ImageId = iff(IsVmss, tostring(coalesce(properties.virtualMachineProfile.storageProfile.imageReference.id, properties.virtualMachineProfile.storageProfile.imageReference.sharedGalleryImageId, properties.virtualMachineProfile.storageProfile.imageReference.communityGalleryImageId)), tostring(coalesce(properties.storageProfile.imageReference.id, properties.storageProfile.imageReference.sharedGalleryImageId, properties.storageProfile.imageReference.communityGalleryImageId)))
| extend ImagePublisher = iff(IsVmss, tostring(properties.virtualMachineProfile.storageProfile.imageReference.publisher), tostring(properties.storageProfile.imageReference.publisher))
| extend ImageOffer = iff(IsVmss, tostring(properties.virtualMachineProfile.storageProfile.imageReference.offer), tostring(properties.storageProfile.imageReference.offer))
| extend GuestOsName = tostring(properties.extended.instanceView.osName)
| project ResourceId = id, Name = name, ResourceGroup = resourceGroup, SubscriptionId = subscriptionId,
          Region = location, VmSize, Instances, OsType, OsDiskId, AvSetId, PowerState, ComputePriority, Zone, ImageSku, ImageId, ImagePublisher, ImageOffer, GuestOsName, IsVmss, Tags = tags
| join kind=leftouter (
    resources
    | where type =~ 'microsoft.compute/disks'
    | project OsDiskId = tolower(id), DiskGen = tostring(properties.hyperVGeneration),
              DiskAdeConfigured = tobool(properties.encryptionSettingsCollection.enabled),
              DiskControllers = tostring(properties.supportedCapabilities.diskControllerTypes)
  ) on OsDiskId
| extend VmGeneration = case(
        isnotempty(DiskGen), DiskGen,
        ImageSku has 'gen2' or ImageSku has '-g2', 'V2',
        'Unknown')
| extend Rec = tostring(parse_json('{ ...lifecycle lookup, see note below... }')[VmSize])
| where isnotempty(Rec)
| extend P = split(Rec, '|')
| extend Lifecycle = tostring(P[0]),
         SeriesGroup = tostring(P[1]),
         RetirementDate = tostring(P[2]),
         RecommendedTarget = tostring(P[3]),
         MemoryPreservingTarget = tostring(P[4]),
         TargetVcpu = tostring(P[5]),
         TargetMemoryGB = tostring(P[6]),
         CurrentVcpu = toint(P[7]),
         CurrentMemoryGB = toreal(P[8]),
         WarnCodes = tostring(P[9]),
         AlternateTargets = tostring(P[10]),
         RiEnded = tostring(P[11]),
         Series = tostring(P[12]),
         PriceUpdate = tostring(P[13]),
         MpVcpu = tostring(P[14]),
         MpMemoryGB = tostring(P[15])
| extend RetiresOn = iff(isempty(RetirementDate), datetime(null), todatetime(RetirementDate))
| extend DaysToRetirement = iff(isempty(RetirementDate), toint(-1), datetime_diff('day', todatetime(RetirementDate), now()))
| extend RegionId = tostring(parse_json('{ ...lifecycle lookup, see note below... }')[tolower(Region)])
| extend TargetRegions = tostring(parse_json('{ ...lifecycle lookup, see note below... }')[RecommendedTarget])
| extend TargetInRegion = case(
        isempty(RecommendedTarget) or isempty(TargetRegions), 'Unverified',
        isempty(RegionId), 'Unverified',
        TargetRegions has strcat('|', RegionId, '|'), 'Yes',
        'No')
| extend PriceUpdate2027 = iff(PriceUpdate == '1', 'Yes', 'No')
// Reserved Instance purchases and renewals for these series ended 2026-07-01.
// This series-wide cutoff is not a customer's reservation expiry or billing coverage.
| extend RiEndsOn = iff(isempty(RiEnded), datetime(null), todatetime(RiEnded))
| extend RiStatus = case(
        isempty(RiEnded), 'No cutoff in captured catalogue; coverage not assessed',
        todatetime(RiEnded) < now(), 'Series purchase/renewal cutoff passed; coverage not assessed',
        'Series purchase/renewal cutoff upcoming; coverage not assessed')
// ---- readiness penalties, evaluated against the REAL fleet attributes ----
| extend NeedsGen2 = iff(WarnCodes has 'g' and VmGeneration == 'V1', 1, 0)
| extend GenUnknown = iff(WarnCodes has 'g' and VmGeneration == 'Unknown', 1, 0)
| extend PenGen = iff(NeedsGen2 == 1, 45, iff(GenUnknown == 1, 20, 0))
| extend PenArch = iff(WarnCodes has 'a', 50, 0)
| extend PenMem = iff(WarnCodes has 'm', 25, 0)
| extend PenTemp = iff(WarnCodes has 't', 20, 0)
| extend PenNvme = iff(WarnCodes has 'n', 15, 0)
| extend PenNarrow = iff(WarnCodes has 'r', 10, 0)
| extend PenAz = iff(WarnCodes has 'z' and isnotempty(Zone), 10, 0)
| extend PenVcpu = iff(WarnCodes has 'v', 5, 0)
| extend PenRegion = iff(TargetInRegion == 'No', 30, 0)
| extend PenAvSet = iff(isnotempty(AvSetId), 5, 0)
| extend PenVmss = iff(IsVmss, 5, 0)
| extend ReadinessScore = toint(iff(100 - PenGen - PenArch - PenMem - PenTemp - PenNvme - PenNarrow - PenAz - PenVcpu - PenRegion - PenAvSet - PenVmss < 0, 0, 100 - PenGen - PenArch - PenMem - PenTemp - PenNvme - PenNarrow - PenAz - PenVcpu - PenRegion - PenAvSet - PenVmss))
| extend Complexity = case(
        ReadinessScore >= 85, 'Few scored platform changes - validation required',
        ReadinessScore >= 60, 'Moderate scored platform changes',
        ReadinessScore >= 35, 'Substantial scored platform changes',
        'Major scored platform changes')
| extend MigrationApproval = 'Not assessed - guest, application, HA, backup and rollback evidence required'
| extend Urgency = case(
        DaysToRetirement < 0, 'No retirement date',
        DaysToRetirement <= 180, 'Critical - under 6 months',
        DaysToRetirement <= 365, 'High - under 12 months',
        DaysToRetirement <= 730, 'Medium - under 24 months',
        'Planned - over 24 months')
| extend ActionCategory = case(
        Lifecycle == 'RETIRE', 'Retirement announced - action required',
        Lifecycle == 'EOL', 'End of life - plan migration',
        'Optional modernization')
| extend OfficialLifecycleStage = case(
        Lifecycle == 'EOL', 'End of life - captured announcement',
        'Unknown - reconcile official Current/Extended/End-of-life/Retired stage; announcement alone is not stage proof')
| extend Blockers = trim(', ', strcat(
        iff(NeedsGen2 == 1, 'Gen1 to Gen2 conversion, ', ''),
        iff(GenUnknown == 1, 'Confirm VM generation, ', ''),
        iff(PenArch > 0, 'Arm64 port, ', ''),
        iff(PenMem > 0, 'Memory downgrade, ', ''),
        iff(PenTemp > 0, 'Temp disk dependency, ', ''),
        iff(PenNvme > 0, 'NVMe boot validation, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'h', 'MANA/accelerated networking validation, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'u', 'No suitable v5 target - manual review, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'k', 'No suitable target in this path - manual selection, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'l', 'Target local-disk capacity unverified, ', ''),
        iff(PenRegion > 0, 'Target not in region, ', ''),
        iff(PenVcpu > 0, 'vCPU count change, ', '')))
| extend Blockers = iff(isempty(Blockers), 'None identified', Blockers)
// Size-level considerations, independent of any individual VM's configuration.
// Used by the size-by-size mapping so rows do not split on per-VM attributes.
| extend SizeConsiderations = trim(', ', strcat(
        iff(WarnCodes has 'g', 'Target is Gen2-only, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'a', 'Arm64 architecture change, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'm', 'Memory reduction at same vCPU, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 't', 'Local temp disk removed, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'n', 'Target requires NVMe boot validation, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'h', 'Hardware-dependent MANA validation, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'u', 'No suitable v5 target - manual review, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'k', 'No suitable target in this path - manual selection, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'l', 'Target local-disk capacity unverified, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'r', 'Narrow target region footprint, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'z', 'Limited zone coverage, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'v', 'No target at current vCPU count, ', '')))
| extend SizeConsiderations = iff(isempty(SizeConsiderations), 'Straight resize', SizeConsiderations)
| where Lifecycle != 'OPTIONAL'
| summarize Resources = count(), Instances = sum(Instances) by VmGeneration
| order by Instances desc
```

### `ov-complexity`

Resource and instance counts by populated scored platform-change band, with average platform score; not workload complexity or approval.

- **Visualization:** table
- **Scope:** `{Subscriptions}`
- **Base:** appended to the shared base query above

```kusto

| where type =~ 'microsoft.compute/virtualmachines' or type =~ 'microsoft.compute/virtualmachinescalesets'

// sku.capacity) and every member VM as separate Resource Graph rows. Counting
// both double-reports the fleet, so member VMs are dropped here and the scale
// set alone represents them. Uniform scale sets do not expose member VMs at

// matters for most modern fleets.

| extend OsType = iff(IsVmss, tostring(properties.virtualMachineProfile.storageProfile.osDisk.osType), tostring(properties.storageProfile.osDisk.osType))
| extend OsDiskId = tolower(tostring(properties.storageProfile.osDisk.managedDisk.id))
| extend AvSetId = tostring(properties.availabilitySet.id)
| extend PowerState = tostring(split(tostring(properties.extended.instanceView.powerState.code), '/')[1])

| extend Zone = tostring(zones[0])

| extend ImageId = iff(IsVmss, tostring(coalesce(properties.virtualMachineProfile.storageProfile.imageReference.id, properties.virtualMachineProfile.storageProfile.imageReference.sharedGalleryImageId, properties.virtualMachineProfile.storageProfile.imageReference.communityGalleryImageId)), tostring(coalesce(properties.storageProfile.imageReference.id, properties.storageProfile.imageReference.sharedGalleryImageId, properties.storageProfile.imageReference.communityGalleryImageId)))
| extend ImagePublisher = iff(IsVmss, tostring(properties.virtualMachineProfile.storageProfile.imageReference.publisher), tostring(properties.storageProfile.imageReference.publisher))
| extend ImageOffer = iff(IsVmss, tostring(properties.virtualMachineProfile.storageProfile.imageReference.offer), tostring(properties.storageProfile.imageReference.offer))
| extend GuestOsName = tostring(properties.extended.instanceView.osName)
| project ResourceId = id, Name = name, ResourceGroup = resourceGroup, SubscriptionId = subscriptionId,
          Region = location, VmSize, Instances, OsType, OsDiskId, AvSetId, PowerState, ComputePriority, Zone, ImageSku, ImageId, ImagePublisher, ImageOffer, GuestOsName, IsVmss, Tags = tags
| join kind=leftouter (
    resources
    | where type =~ 'microsoft.compute/disks'
    | project OsDiskId = tolower(id), DiskGen = tostring(properties.hyperVGeneration),
              DiskAdeConfigured = tobool(properties.encryptionSettingsCollection.enabled),
              DiskControllers = tostring(properties.supportedCapabilities.diskControllerTypes)
  ) on OsDiskId
| extend VmGeneration = case(
        isnotempty(DiskGen), DiskGen,
        ImageSku has 'gen2' or ImageSku has '-g2', 'V2',
        'Unknown')
| extend Rec = tostring(parse_json('{ ...lifecycle lookup, see note below... }')[VmSize])
| where isnotempty(Rec)
| extend P = split(Rec, '|')
| extend Lifecycle = tostring(P[0]),
         SeriesGroup = tostring(P[1]),
         RetirementDate = tostring(P[2]),
         RecommendedTarget = tostring(P[3]),
         MemoryPreservingTarget = tostring(P[4]),
         TargetVcpu = tostring(P[5]),
         TargetMemoryGB = tostring(P[6]),
         CurrentVcpu = toint(P[7]),
         CurrentMemoryGB = toreal(P[8]),
         WarnCodes = tostring(P[9]),
         AlternateTargets = tostring(P[10]),
         RiEnded = tostring(P[11]),
         Series = tostring(P[12]),
         PriceUpdate = tostring(P[13]),
         MpVcpu = tostring(P[14]),
         MpMemoryGB = tostring(P[15])
| extend RetiresOn = iff(isempty(RetirementDate), datetime(null), todatetime(RetirementDate))
| extend DaysToRetirement = iff(isempty(RetirementDate), toint(-1), datetime_diff('day', todatetime(RetirementDate), now()))
| extend RegionId = tostring(parse_json('{ ...lifecycle lookup, see note below... }')[tolower(Region)])
| extend TargetRegions = tostring(parse_json('{ ...lifecycle lookup, see note below... }')[RecommendedTarget])

        TargetRegions has strcat('|', RegionId, '|'), 'Yes',
        'No')
| extend PriceUpdate2027 = iff(PriceUpdate == '1', 'Yes', 'No')
// Reserved Instance purchases and renewals for these series ended 2026-07-01.
// This series-wide cutoff is not a customer's reservation expiry or billing coverage.
| extend RiEndsOn = iff(isempty(RiEnded), datetime(null), todatetime(RiEnded))
| extend RiStatus = case(

| extend PenVmss = iff(IsVmss, 5, 0)
| extend ReadinessScore = toint(iff(100 - PenGen - PenArch - PenMem - PenTemp - PenNvme - PenNarrow - PenAz - PenVcpu - PenRegion - PenAvSet - PenVmss < 0, 0, 100 - PenGen - PenArch - PenMem - PenTemp - PenNvme - PenNarrow - PenAz - PenVcpu - PenRegion - PenAvSet - PenVmss))

        ReadinessScore >= 85, 'Few scored platform changes - validation required',
        ReadinessScore >= 60, 'Moderate scored platform changes',

        'Major scored platform changes')
| extend MigrationApproval = 'Not assessed - guest, application, HA, backup and rollback evidence required'
| extend Urgency = case(
        DaysToRetirement < 0, 'No retirement date',
        DaysToRetirement <= 180, 'Critical - under 6 months',
        DaysToRetirement <= 365, 'High - under 12 months',
        DaysToRetirement <= 730, 'Medium - under 24 months',
        'Planned - over 24 months')
| extend ActionCategory = case(
        Lifecycle == 'RETIRE', 'Retirement announced - action required',
        Lifecycle == 'EOL', 'End of life - plan migration',
        'Optional modernization')
| extend OfficialLifecycleStage = case(
        Lifecycle == 'EOL', 'End of life - captured announcement',
        'Unknown - reconcile official Current/Extended/End-of-life/Retired stage; announcement alone is not stage proof')

        iff(WarnCodes has 'u', 'No suitable v5 target - manual review, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'k', 'No suitable target in this path - manual selection, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'l', 'Target local-disk capacity unverified, ', ''),
        iff(PenRegion > 0, 'Target not in region, ', ''),
        iff(PenVcpu > 0, 'vCPU count change, ', '')))

// Used by the size-by-size mapping so rows do not split on per-VM attributes.
| extend SizeConsiderations = trim(', ', strcat(
        iff(WarnCodes has 'g', 'Target is Gen2-only, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'a', 'Arm64 architecture change, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'm', 'Memory reduction at same vCPU, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 't', 'Local temp disk removed, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'n', 'Target requires NVMe boot validation, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'h', 'Hardware-dependent MANA validation, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'u', 'No suitable v5 target - manual review, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'k', 'No suitable target in this path - manual selection, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'l', 'Target local-disk capacity unverified, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'r', 'Narrow target region footprint, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'z', 'Limited zone coverage, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'v', 'No target at current vCPU count, ', '')))
| extend SizeConsiderations = iff(isempty(SizeConsiderations), 'Straight resize', SizeConsiderations)
| where Lifecycle != 'OPTIONAL'
| summarize Resources = count(), Instances = sum(Instances), AvgReadiness = round(avg(todouble(ReadinessScore)), 0) by Complexity
| order by AvgReadiness asc
```

### `ov-commercial`

         MpVcpu = tostring(P[14]),
         MpMemoryGB = tostring(P[15])

| extend DaysToRetirement = iff(isempty(RetirementDate), toint(-1), datetime_diff('day', todatetime(RetirementDate), now()))

| extend ReadinessScore = toint(iff(100 - PenGen - PenArch - PenMem - PenTemp - PenNvme - PenNarrow - PenAz - PenVcpu - PenRegion - PenAvSet - PenVmss < 0, 0, 100 - PenGen - PenArch - PenMem - PenTemp - PenNvme - PenNarrow - PenAz - PenVcpu - PenRegion - PenAvSet - PenVmss))

        'Major scored platform changes')
| extend MigrationApproval = 'Not assessed - guest, application, HA, backup and rollback evidence required'
| extend Urgency = case(
        DaysToRetirement < 0, 'No retirement date',
        DaysToRetirement <= 180, 'Critical - under 6 months',
        DaysToRetirement <= 365, 'High - under 12 months',
        DaysToRetirement <= 730, 'Medium - under 24 months',
        'Planned - over 24 months')
| extend ActionCategory = case(
        Lifecycle == 'RETIRE', 'Retirement announced - action required',
        Lifecycle == 'EOL', 'End of life - plan migration',
        'Optional modernization')
| extend OfficialLifecycleStage = case(
        Lifecycle == 'EOL', 'End of life - captured announcement',
        'Unknown - reconcile official Current/Extended/End-of-life/Retired stage; announcement alone is not stage proof')
| extend Blockers = trim(', ', strcat(
        iff(NeedsGen2 == 1, 'Gen1 to Gen2 conversion, ', ''),
        iff(GenUnknown == 1, 'Confirm VM generation, ', ''),
        iff(PenArch > 0, 'Arm64 port, ', ''),
        iff(PenMem > 0, 'Memory downgrade, ', ''),
        iff(PenTemp > 0, 'Temp disk dependency, ', ''),
        iff(PenNvme > 0, 'NVMe boot validation, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'h', 'MANA/accelerated networking validation, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'u', 'No suitable v5 target - manual review, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'k', 'No suitable target in this path - manual selection, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'l', 'Target local-disk capacity unverified, ', ''),
        iff(PenRegion > 0, 'Target not in region, ', ''),
        iff(PenVcpu > 0, 'vCPU count change, ', '')))
| extend Blockers = iff(isempty(Blockers), 'None identified', Blockers)
// Size-level considerations, independent of any individual VM's configuration.
// Used by the size-by-size mapping so rows do not split on per-VM attributes.
| extend SizeConsiderations = trim(', ', strcat(
        iff(WarnCodes has 'g', 'Target is Gen2-only, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'a', 'Arm64 architecture change, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'm', 'Memory reduction at same vCPU, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 't', 'Local temp disk removed, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'n', 'Target requires NVMe boot validation, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'h', 'Hardware-dependent MANA validation, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'u', 'No suitable v5 target - manual review, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'k', 'No suitable target in this path - manual selection, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'l', 'Target local-disk capacity unverified, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'r', 'Narrow target region footprint, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'z', 'Limited zone coverage, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'v', 'No target at current vCPU count, ', '')))
| extend SizeConsiderations = iff(isempty(SizeConsiderations), 'Straight resize', SizeConsiderations)
| where Lifecycle != 'OPTIONAL'
| summarize Instances = sum(Instances) by Series, PriceUpdate2027, RiStatus
| where PriceUpdate2027 == 'Yes' or RiStatus startswith 'Series'
| order by Instances desc
```

### `ov-topsizes`

The 25 most-used affected sizes with their recommended target and next retirement date.

- **Visualization:** table
- **Scope:** `{Subscriptions}`
- **Base:** appended to the shared base query above

```kusto
resources
| where type =~ 'microsoft.compute/virtualmachines' or type =~ 'microsoft.compute/virtualmachinescalesets'
| extend IsVmss = (type =~ 'microsoft.compute/virtualmachinescalesets')
// A Flexible-orchestration scale set surfaces BOTH the scale set (carrying
// sku.capacity) and every member VM as separate Resource Graph rows. Counting
// both double-reports the fleet, so member VMs are dropped here and the scale
// set alone represents them. Uniform scale sets do not expose member VMs at
// all, so they are unaffected. Flexible is the current Azure default, so this
// matters for most modern fleets.

| where IsVmss or isempty(tostring(properties.virtualMachineScaleSet.id))
| extend VmSize = iff(IsVmss, tostring(sku.name), tostring(properties.hardwareProfile.vmSize))
| extend Instances = iff(IsVmss, toint(sku.capacity), 1)
| extend OsType = iff(IsVmss, tostring(properties.virtualMachineProfile.storageProfile.osDisk.osType), tostring(properties.storageProfile.osDisk.osType))
| extend OsDiskId = tolower(tostring(properties.storageProfile.osDisk.managedDisk.id))
| extend AvSetId = tostring(properties.availabilitySet.id)
| extend PowerState = tostring(split(tostring(properties.extended.instanceView.powerState.code), '/')[1])
| extend ComputePriority = iff(IsVmss, tostring(properties.virtualMachineProfile.priority), tostring(properties.priority))
| extend Zone = tostring(zones[0])
| extend ImageSku = iff(IsVmss, tostring(properties.virtualMachineProfile.storageProfile.imageReference.sku), tostring(properties.storageProfile.imageReference.sku))
| extend ImageId = iff(IsVmss, tostring(coalesce(properties.virtualMachineProfile.storageProfile.imageReference.id, properties.virtualMachineProfile.storageProfile.imageReference.sharedGalleryImageId, properties.virtualMachineProfile.storageProfile.imageReference.communityGalleryImageId)), tostring(coalesce(properties.storageProfile.imageReference.id, properties.storageProfile.imageReference.sharedGalleryImageId, properties.storageProfile.imageReference.communityGalleryImageId)))
| extend ImagePublisher = iff(IsVmss, tostring(properties.virtualMachineProfile.storageProfile.imageReference.publisher), tostring(properties.storageProfile.imageReference.publisher))
| extend ImageOffer = iff(IsVmss, tostring(properties.virtualMachineProfile.storageProfile.imageReference.offer), tostring(properties.storageProfile.imageReference.offer))
| extend GuestOsName = tostring(properties.extended.instanceView.osName)
| project ResourceId = id, Name = name, ResourceGroup = resourceGroup, SubscriptionId = subscriptionId,
          Region = location, VmSize, Instances, OsType, OsDiskId, AvSetId, PowerState, ComputePriority, Zone, ImageSku, ImageId, ImagePublisher, ImageOffer, GuestOsName, IsVmss, Tags = tags
| join kind=leftouter (
    resources
    | where type =~ 'microsoft.compute/disks'
    | project OsDiskId = tolower(id), DiskGen = tostring(properties.hyperVGeneration),
              DiskAdeConfigured = tobool(properties.encryptionSettingsCollection.enabled),
              DiskControllers = tostring(properties.supportedCapabilities.diskControllerTypes)
  ) on OsDiskId
| extend VmGeneration = case(
        isnotempty(DiskGen), DiskGen,
        ImageSku has 'gen2' or ImageSku has '-g2', 'V2',
        'Unknown')
| extend Rec = tostring(parse_json('{ ...lifecycle lookup, see note below... }')[VmSize])
| where isnotempty(Rec)
| extend P = split(Rec, '|')
| extend Lifecycle = tostring(P[0]),
         SeriesGroup = tostring(P[1]),
         RetirementDate = tostring(P[2]),
         RecommendedTarget = tostring(P[3]),
         MemoryPreservingTarget = tostring(P[4]),
         TargetVcpu = tostring(P[5]),
         TargetMemoryGB = tostring(P[6]),
         CurrentVcpu = toint(P[7]),
         CurrentMemoryGB = toreal(P[8]),
         WarnCodes = tostring(P[9]),
         AlternateTargets = tostring(P[10]),
         RiEnded = tostring(P[11]),
         Series = tostring(P[12]),
         PriceUpdate = tostring(P[13]),
         MpVcpu = tostring(P[14]),
         MpMemoryGB = tostring(P[15])
| extend RetiresOn = iff(isempty(RetirementDate), datetime(null), todatetime(RetirementDate))
| extend DaysToRetirement = iff(isempty(RetirementDate), toint(-1), datetime_diff('day', todatetime(RetirementDate), now()))
| extend RegionId = tostring(parse_json('{ ...lifecycle lookup, see note below... }')[tolower(Region)])
| extend TargetRegions = tostring(parse_json('{ ...lifecycle lookup, see note below... }')[RecommendedTarget])
| extend TargetInRegion = case(
        isempty(RecommendedTarget) or isempty(TargetRegions), 'Unverified',
        isempty(RegionId), 'Unverified',
        TargetRegions has strcat('|', RegionId, '|'), 'Yes',
        'No')
| extend PriceUpdate2027 = iff(PriceUpdate == '1', 'Yes', 'No')
// Reserved Instance purchases and renewals for these series ended 2026-07-01.
// This series-wide cutoff is not a customer's reservation expiry or billing coverage.
| extend RiEndsOn = iff(isempty(RiEnded), datetime(null), todatetime(RiEnded))
| extend RiStatus = case(
        isempty(RiEnded), 'No cutoff in captured catalogue; coverage not assessed',
        todatetime(RiEnded) < now(), 'Series purchase/renewal cutoff passed; coverage not assessed',
        'Series purchase/renewal cutoff upcoming; coverage not assessed')
// ---- readiness penalties, evaluated against the REAL fleet attributes ----
| extend NeedsGen2 = iff(WarnCodes has 'g' and VmGeneration == 'V1', 1, 0)
| extend GenUnknown = iff(WarnCodes has 'g' and VmGeneration == 'Unknown', 1, 0)
| extend PenGen = iff(NeedsGen2 == 1, 45, iff(GenUnknown == 1, 20, 0))
| extend PenArch = iff(WarnCodes has 'a', 50, 0)
| extend PenMem = iff(WarnCodes has 'm', 25, 0)
| extend PenTemp = iff(WarnCodes has 't', 20, 0)
| extend PenNvme = iff(WarnCodes has 'n', 15, 0)
| extend PenNarrow = iff(WarnCodes has 'r', 10, 0)
| extend PenAz = iff(WarnCodes has 'z' and isnotempty(Zone), 10, 0)
| extend PenVcpu = iff(WarnCodes has 'v', 5, 0)
| extend PenRegion = iff(TargetInRegion == 'No', 30, 0)
| extend PenAvSet = iff(isnotempty(AvSetId), 5, 0)
| extend PenVmss = iff(IsVmss, 5, 0)
| extend ReadinessScore = toint(iff(100 - PenGen - PenArch - PenMem - PenTemp - PenNvme - PenNarrow - PenAz - PenVcpu - PenRegion - PenAvSet - PenVmss < 0, 0, 100 - PenGen - PenArch - PenMem - PenTemp - PenNvme - PenNarrow - PenAz - PenVcpu - PenRegion - PenAvSet - PenVmss))
| extend Complexity = case(
        ReadinessScore >= 85, 'Few scored platform changes - validation required',
        ReadinessScore >= 60, 'Moderate scored platform changes',
        ReadinessScore >= 35, 'Substantial scored platform changes',
        'Major scored platform changes')
| extend MigrationApproval = 'Not assessed - guest, application, HA, backup and rollback evidence required'
| extend Urgency = case(
        DaysToRetirement < 0, 'No retirement date',
        DaysToRetirement <= 180, 'Critical - under 6 months',
        DaysToRetirement <= 365, 'High - under 12 months',
        DaysToRetirement <= 730, 'Medium - under 24 months',
        'Planned - over 24 months')
| extend ActionCategory = case(
        Lifecycle == 'RETIRE', 'Retirement announced - action required',
        Lifecycle == 'EOL', 'End of life - plan migration',
        'Optional modernization')
| extend OfficialLifecycleStage = case(
        Lifecycle == 'EOL', 'End of life - captured announcement',
        'Unknown - reconcile official Current/Extended/End-of-life/Retired stage; announcement alone is not stage proof')
| extend Blockers = trim(', ', strcat(
        iff(NeedsGen2 == 1, 'Gen1 to Gen2 conversion, ', ''),
        iff(GenUnknown == 1, 'Confirm VM generation, ', ''),
        iff(PenArch > 0, 'Arm64 port, ', ''),
        iff(PenMem > 0, 'Memory downgrade, ', ''),
        iff(PenTemp > 0, 'Temp disk dependency, ', ''),
        iff(PenNvme > 0, 'NVMe boot validation, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'h', 'MANA/accelerated networking validation, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'u', 'No suitable v5 target - manual review, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'k', 'No suitable target in this path - manual selection, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'l', 'Target local-disk capacity unverified, ', ''),
        iff(PenRegion > 0, 'Target not in region, ', ''),
        iff(PenVcpu > 0, 'vCPU count change, ', '')))
| extend Blockers = iff(isempty(Blockers), 'None identified', Blockers)
// Size-level considerations, independent of any individual VM's configuration.
// Used by the size-by-size mapping so rows do not split on per-VM attributes.
| extend SizeConsiderations = trim(', ', strcat(
        iff(WarnCodes has 'g', 'Target is Gen2-only, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'a', 'Arm64 architecture change, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'm', 'Memory reduction at same vCPU, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 't', 'Local temp disk removed, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'n', 'Target requires NVMe boot validation, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'h', 'Hardware-dependent MANA validation, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'u', 'No suitable v5 target - manual review, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'k', 'No suitable target in this path - manual selection, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'l', 'Target local-disk capacity unverified, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'r', 'Narrow target region footprint, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'z', 'Limited zone coverage, ', ''),
        iff(WarnCodes has 'v', 'No target at current vCPU count, ', '')))
| extend SizeConsiderations = iff(isempty(SizeConsiderations), 'Straight resize', SizeConsiderations)
| where Lifecycle != 'OPTIONAL'
| summarize Resources = count(), Instances = sum(Instances), AvgReadiness = round(avg(todouble(ReadinessScore)), 0),
            NextRetirement = min(RetiresOn)
    by VmSize, Series, ActionCategory, RecommendedTarget
| order by Instances desc
| take 25
```

## Inventory - Retirement and pricing

### `imp-grid`

          Blockers, MigrationApproval, ResourceId, Lifecycle, OfficialLifecycleStage

### `regional-price-grid`

Independent inventory of VMs and scale sets in seven regions with publicly reported pricing changes; potential exposure and reported exclusions, not a confirmed bill forecast.

- **Visualization:** table
- **Scope:** `{Subscriptions}`
- **Base:** appended to the shared base query above

```kusto
resources
| where type =~ 'microsoft.compute/virtualmachines' or type =~ 'microsoft.compute/virtualmachinescalesets'
| extend IsVmss = (type =~ 'microsoft.compute/virtualmachinescalesets')
// A Flexible-orchestration scale set surfaces BOTH the scale set (carrying
// sku.capacity) and every member VM as separate Resource Graph rows. Counting
// both double-reports the fleet, so member VMs are dropped here and the scale
// set alone represents them. Uniform scale sets do not expose member VMs at
// all, so they are unaffected. Flexible is the current Azure default, so this
// matters for most modern fleets.

| where IsVmss or isempty(tostring(properties.virtualMachineScaleSet.id))
| extend VmSize = iff(IsVmss, tostring(sku.name), tostring(properties.hardwareProfile.vmSize))
| extend Instances = iff(IsVmss, toint(sku.capacity), 1)
| extend OsType = iff(IsVmss, tostring(properties.virtualMachineProfile.storageProfile.osDisk.osType), tostring(properties.storageProfile.osDisk.osType))
| extend OsDiskId = tolower(tostring(properties.storageProfile.osDisk.managedDisk.id))
| extend AvSetId = tostring(properties.availabilitySet.id)
| extend PowerState = tostring(split(tostring(properties.extended.instanceView.powerState.code), '/')[1])
| extend ComputePriority = iff(IsVmss, tostring(properties.virtualMachineProfile.priority), tostring(properties.priority))
| extend Zone = tostring(zones[0])
| extend ImageSku = iff(IsVmss, tostring(properties.virtualMachineProfile.storageProfile.imageReference.sku), tostring(properties.storageProfile.imageReference.sku))
| extend ImageId = iff(IsVmss, tostring(coalesce(properties.virtualMachineProfile.storageProfile.imageReference.id, properties.virtualMachineProfile.storageProfile.imageReference.sharedGalleryImageId, properties.virtualMachineProfile.storageProfile.imageReference.communityGalleryImageId)), tostring(coalesce(properties.storageProfile.imageReference.id, properties.storageProfile.imageReference.sharedGalleryImageId, properties.storageProfile.imageReference.communityGalleryImageId)))
| extend ImagePublisher = iff(IsVmss, tostring(properties.virtualMachineProfile.storageProfile.imageReference.publisher), tostring(properties.storageProfile.imageReference.publisher))
| extend ImageOffer = iff(IsVmss, tostring(properties.virtualMachineProfile.storageProfile.imageReference.offer), tostring(properties.storageProfile.imageReference.offer))
| extend GuestOsName = tostring(properties.extended.instanceView.osName)
| project ResourceId = id, Name = name, ResourceGroup = resourceGroup, SubscriptionId = subscriptionId,
          Region = location, VmSize, Instances, OsType, OsDiskId, AvSetId, PowerState, ComputePriority, Zone, ImageSku, ImageId, ImagePublisher, ImageOffer, GuestOsName, IsVmss, Tags = tags
| extend AnnouncedRegionalPercent = toint(parse_json('{ ...lifecycle lookup, see note below... }')[tolower(Region)])
| where isnotnull(AnnouncedRegionalPercent)
| extend Series = tostring(parse_json('{ ...lifecycle lookup, see note below... }')[tolower(VmSize)])
| extend ReportedExcluded = tobool(parse_json('{ ...lifecycle lookup, see note below... }')[tolower(Series)]) == true
        or tolower(VmSize) matches regex '^standard_(d[0-9]+(-[0-9]+)?(s|ds|ls|lds)|e[0-9]+(-[0-9]+)?(s|ds))_v7$'
| extend Exposure = case(
        ReportedExcluded == true, 'Reported regional exclusion - not a universal exemption',
        isempty(Series), 'Potential exposure - series/scope unknown',
        'Potential exposure - verify advisory and agreement')
| extend EffectiveDate = datetime(2027-02-01),
         Evidence = 'Public Q&A reproduction of customer notification; not a formal public announcement',
         Scope = 'VM compute only; not a bill estimate. Confirm meters, contract, reservations and regional scope in Service Health. Separate legacy-series changes may apply.',
         Source = 'https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/answers/questions/6025662/is-there-any-official-announcement-for-azure-vm-in'
| project Name, ResourceGroup, Region, VmSize, Instances, Series, Exposure,
          AnnouncedRegionalPercent, EffectiveDate, Evidence, Scope, Source, ResourceId
| order by AnnouncedRegionalPercent desc, Region asc, Name asc
```

## Inventory - Workloads and owners

### `discovery-compute`

Compute ownership and resource evidence; NIC/disk visibility, SQL/SAP signals, service-specific migration methods and conservative OS hints. Flexible member details are not additive to model capacity.

- **Visualization:** table
- **Scope:** `{Subscriptions}`
- **Base:** appended to the shared base query above

```kusto
resources
| where type in~ ('microsoft.compute/virtualmachines', 'microsoft.compute/virtualmachinescalesets',
    'microsoft.network/networkinterfaces', 'microsoft.compute/disks',
    'microsoft.compute/virtualmachines/extensions', 'microsoft.sqlvirtualmachine/sqlvirtualmachines')
| extend IsVmss = type =~ 'microsoft.compute/virtualmachinescalesets',
         IsCompute = type in~ ('microsoft.compute/virtualmachines', 'microsoft.compute/virtualmachinescalesets'),
         IsNic = type =~ 'microsoft.network/networkinterfaces', IsDisk = type =~ 'microsoft.compute/disks',
         IsRegistration = type =~ 'microsoft.sqlvirtualmachine/sqlvirtualmachines',
         IsExtension = type =~ 'microsoft.compute/virtualmachines/extensions'
| extend IsSqlExtension = IsExtension and tostring(properties.publisher) =~ 'Microsoft.SqlServer.Management'
            and (tostring(properties.type) =~ 'SqlIaaSAgent' or tostring(properties.typePropertiesType) =~ 'SqlIaaSAgent'),
         IsAdeExtension = IsExtension and tostring(properties.publisher) in~ ('Microsoft.Azure.Security', 'Microsoft.Azure.Security.Edp')
            and tostring(properties.type) in~ ('AzureDiskEncryption', 'AzureDiskEncryptionForLinux'),
         IsSapExtension = IsExtension and tostring(properties.publisher) in~ ('Microsoft.AzureCAT.AzureEnhancedMonitoring', 'Microsoft.OSTCExtensions')
            and tostring(properties.type) in~ ('MonitorX64Windows', 'MonitorX64Linux', 'AzureEnhancedMonitorForLinux', 'AzureCATExtensionHandler')
| extend ComputeProfile = iff(IsVmss, properties.virtualMachineProfile, properties)
| extend Keys = case(IsCompute, array_concat(pack_array(id),
            coalesce(ComputeProfile.storageProfile.dataDisks, dynamic([])),
            pack_array(tostring(ComputeProfile.storageProfile.osDisk.managedDisk.id))),
        IsNic, pack_array(tostring(properties.virtualMachine.id)),
        IsRegistration, pack_array(tostring(properties.virtualMachineResourceId)),
        IsExtension, pack_array(tostring(split(tolower(id), '/extensions/')[0])),
        IsDisk, pack_array(id), dynamic([]))
| mv-expand Key = Keys limit 2000
| extend VmId = tolower(iff(isnotempty(tostring(Key.managedDisk.id)), tostring(Key.managedDisk.id), tostring(Key)))
| where isnotempty(VmId)
| extend IsSelf = IsCompute and tolower(id) == VmId
| summarize ComputeRows = countif(IsSelf),
        VM = take_anyif(pack('id', id, 'type', type, 'name', name, 'resourceGroup', resourceGroup, 'subscriptionId', subscriptionId,
            'location', location, 'properties', properties, 'sku', sku, 'tags', tags,
            'identity', identity, 'plan', plan, 'managedBy', managedBy, 'zones', zones), IsSelf),
        AttachedVmIds = make_set_if(tolower(id), IsCompute and not(IsSelf), 2000),
        NicRows = countif(IsNic), AcceleratedNicRows = countif(IsNic and tobool(properties.enableAcceleratedNetworking) == true),
        NicFlagRows = countif(IsNic and isnotnull(tobool(properties.enableAcceleratedNetworking))),
        DiskRows = countif(IsDisk), SharedDiskRows = countif(IsDisk and toint(properties.maxShares) > 1),
        AdeDiskRows = countif(IsDisk and tobool(properties.encryptionSettingsCollection.enabled) == true),
        DiskIops = sumif(tolong(properties.diskIOPSReadWrite), IsDisk),
        DiskMBps = sumif(toreal(properties.diskMBpsReadWrite), IsDisk),
        DiskIopsKnown = countif(IsDisk and isnotnull(tolong(properties.diskIOPSReadWrite))),
        DiskMBpsKnown = countif(IsDisk and isnotnull(toreal(properties.diskMBpsReadWrite))),
        AdeExtensionRows = countif(IsAdeExtension), SapExtensionRows = countif(IsSapExtension),
        SqlExtensionRows = countif(IsSqlExtension), SqlRegistrationRows = countif(IsRegistration),
        SqlGroupIds = make_set_if(tostring(properties.sqlVirtualMachineGroupResourceId), IsRegistration, 20)
    by VmId
| extend ComputeKeys = iff(ComputeRows > 0, pack_array(VmId), AttachedVmIds)
| mv-expand ComputeKey = ComputeKeys limit 2000
| summarize VM = take_anyif(VM, ComputeRows > 0), ComputeRows = sum(ComputeRows),
        NicRows = sum(NicRows), AcceleratedNicRows = sum(AcceleratedNicRows), NicFlagRows = sum(NicFlagRows),
        DiskRows = sum(DiskRows), SharedDiskRows = sum(SharedDiskRows), AdeExtensionRows = sum(AdeExtensionRows),
        AdeDiskRows = sum(AdeDiskRows), DiskIops = sum(DiskIops), DiskMBps = sum(DiskMBps),
        DiskIopsKnown = sum(DiskIopsKnown), DiskMBpsKnown = sum(DiskMBpsKnown),
        SapExtensionRows = sum(SapExtensionRows), SqlExtensionRows = sum(SqlExtensionRows),
        SqlRegistrationRows = sum(SqlRegistrationRows), SqlGroupIds = take_anyif(SqlGroupIds, ComputeRows > 0)
    by VmId = tostring(ComputeKey)
| where ComputeRows == 1
| extend ResourceId = tostring(VM.id), Name = tostring(VM.name), ResourceGroup = tostring(VM.resourceGroup),
         SubscriptionId = tostring(VM.subscriptionId), Region = tostring(VM.location),
         IsVmss = tostring(VM.type) =~ 'microsoft.compute/virtualmachinescalesets',
         Profile = iff(tostring(VM.type) =~ 'microsoft.compute/virtualmachinescalesets', VM.properties.virtualMachineProfile, VM.properties)
| extend VmSize = iff(IsVmss, tostring(VM.sku.name), tostring(Profile.hardwareProfile.vmSize)),
         RgId = tolower(strcat('/subscriptions/', SubscriptionId, '/resourceGroups/', ResourceGroup)),
         ExpectedNics = array_length(Profile.networkProfile.networkInterfaces),
         DataDiskCount = array_length(Profile.storageProfile.dataDisks),
         ImagePublisher = tostring(Profile.storageProfile.imageReference.publisher),
         ImageOffer = tostring(Profile.storageProfile.imageReference.offer), ImageSku = tostring(Profile.storageProfile.imageReference.sku),
         GuestOsName = tostring(VM.properties.extended.instanceView.osName)
| extend ExpectedDisks = coalesce(DataDiskCount, 0) + iff(isnotempty(tostring(Profile.storageProfile.osDisk.managedDisk.id)), 1, 0)
| extend DiskVisibilityComplete = not(IsVmss) and isnotnull(DataDiskCount) and DiskRows == ExpectedDisks and ExpectedDisks > 0,
         NicVisibilityComplete = not(IsVmss) and isnotnull(ExpectedNics) and NicRows == ExpectedNics,
         PlacementEvidence = strcat('Zones: ', tostring(VM.zones),
             '; Availability set: ', tostring(VM.properties.availabilitySet.id),
             '; Dedicated Host: ', tostring(VM.properties.host.id),
             '; Host group: ', tostring(VM.properties.hostGroup.id),
             '; PPG: ', tostring(VM.properties.proximityPlacementGroup.id),
             '; Capacity reservation: ', tostring(Profile.capacityReservation.capacityReservationGroup.id),
             '; Empty/missing associations do not verify target placement'),
         ApplianceEvidence = iff(ImagePublisher in~ ('fortinet', 'paloaltonetworks', 'cisco',
             'checkpoint', 'barracudanetworks', 'f5-networks', 'sophos', 'netapp', 'veeam',
             'cohesity', 'rubrik') or tostring(VM.plan.publisher) in~ ('fortinet', 'paloaltonetworks',
             'cisco', 'checkpoint', 'barracudanetworks', 'f5-networks', 'sophos', 'netapp',
             'veeam', 'cohesity', 'rubrik'), strcat('Suspected vendor appliance - image/plan publishers: ',
             ImagePublisher, '/', tostring(VM.plan.publisher),
             '; verify exact product/version and target/NIC/driver/HA certification'),
             'Unknown - image/plan and application owner review required; Marketplace alone is not an appliance signal')
| extend ProvisionedDiskIops = iff(DiskVisibilityComplete and DiskIopsKnown == ExpectedDisks, DiskIops, tolong('')),
         ProvisionedDiskMBps = iff(DiskVisibilityComplete and DiskMBpsKnown == ExpectedDisks, DiskMBps, toreal(''))
| extend AcceleratedNetworking = case(IsVmss, 'Review scale-set NIC configurations',
            isnull(ExpectedNics) or NicRows != ExpectedNics or NicFlagRows != NicRows, 'Unknown - incomplete NIC evidence',
            strcat(AcceleratedNicRows, ' of ', ExpectedNics, ' NICs enabled')),
         SharedDiskEvidence = case(SharedDiskRows > 0, 'Detected - review clustering and migration sequencing',
            IsVmss or DiskRows != ExpectedDisks or ExpectedDisks == 0, 'Unknown - incomplete attached-disk evidence',
            'No sharing-enabled attached disks observed'),
         EphemeralOsDisk = case(tostring(Profile.storageProfile.osDisk.diffDiskSettings.option) =~ 'Local', 'Configured - rebuild/persistence review',
            isempty(tostring(Profile.storageProfile.osDisk)), 'Unknown', 'Not configured in indexed model'),
         UnmanagedDiskEvidence = iff(isnotempty(tostring(Profile.storageProfile.osDisk.vhd.uri)) or tostring(Profile.storageProfile.dataDisks) contains 'vhd',
            'Detected - managed-disk migration review', 'No unmanaged disk reference observed'),
         Hibernation = case(tobool(VM.properties.additionalCapabilities.hibernationEnabled) == true, 'Enabled - target support review',
            isnull(VM.properties.additionalCapabilities.hibernationEnabled), 'Unknown - not recorded', 'Disabled in indexed model'),
         SecurityType = iff(isempty(tostring(Profile.securityProfile.securityType)), 'Not recorded - verify effective security type', tostring(Profile.securityProfile.securityType)),
         SystemAssignedIdentity = iff(tostring(VM.identity.type) contains 'SystemAssigned', 'Present - preserve/re-authorize if rebuilding', 'No system-assigned identity recorded'),
         MarketplacePlan = strcat(tostring(VM.plan.publisher), ' / ', tostring(VM.plan.product)),
         AdeEvidence = case(AdeExtensionRows > 0, 'Extension observed - guest encryption state unverified',
             AdeDiskRows > 0, 'Attached-disk ADE configuration observed - confirm OS/data and guest state',
             'Guest encryption state unverified'),
         InventoryRole = case(IsVmss, 'Scale-set model', isnotempty(tostring(Profile.virtualMachineScaleSet.id)), 'Flexible member detail - do not add to model capacity', 'Standalone VM')
| join kind=leftouter (
    resources
    | where type in~ ('microsoft.containerservice/managedclusters', 'microsoft.databricks/workspaces',
        'microsoft.solutions/applications', 'microsoft.redhatopenshift/openshiftclusters')
    | extend OwnerKind = case(type =~ 'microsoft.containerservice/managedclusters', 'AKS',
             type =~ 'microsoft.databricks/workspaces', 'Databricks',
             type =~ 'microsoft.redhatopenshift/openshiftclusters', 'ARO', 'Managed application')
    | extend RgId = tolower(case(OwnerKind == 'AKS', strcat('/subscriptions/', subscriptionId, '/resourceGroups/', tostring(properties.nodeResourceGroup)),
             OwnerKind == 'ARO', tostring(properties.clusterProfile.resourceGroupId), tostring(properties.managedResourceGroupId)))
    | project RgId, OwnerKind, OwnerId = tolower(id)
    | union (
        resourcecontainers
        | where type =~ 'microsoft.resources/subscriptions/resourcegroups' and isnotempty(tostring(managedBy))
        | extend OwnerId = tolower(tostring(managedBy))
        | project RgId = tolower(id), OwnerId, OwnerKind = case(
            OwnerId contains '/microsoft.containerservice/managedclusters/', 'AKS',
            OwnerId contains '/microsoft.databricks/workspaces/', 'Databricks',
            OwnerId contains '/microsoft.batch/', 'Batch', 'Managed service')
      )
    | where isnotempty(RgId) and isnotempty(OwnerId)
    | summarize OwnerIds = make_set(OwnerId, 20), OwnerKinds = make_set(OwnerKind, 20) by RgId
  ) on RgId
| join kind=leftouter (
    desktopvirtualizationresources
    | where type =~ 'microsoft.desktopvirtualization/hostpools/sessionhosts'
    | extend VmId = tolower(tostring(properties.resourceId))
    | where isnotempty(VmId)
    | summarize AvdHostPoolIds = make_set(tostring(split(tolower(id), '/sessionhosts/')[0]), 20) by VmId
  ) on VmId
| extend Workload = trim('; ', strcat(
        iff(array_length(OwnerKinds) > 0, strcat(strcat_array(OwnerKinds, '; '), '; '), ''),
        iff(isnotempty(tostring(VM.managedBy)) and coalesce(array_length(OwnerKinds), 0) == 0, 'Managed service (compute managedBy); ', ''),
        iff(array_length(AvdHostPoolIds) > 0, 'AVD; ', ''),
        iff(SqlExtensionRows > 0 or SqlRegistrationRows > 0, 'SQL; ', ''),
        iff(SapExtensionRows > 0 or tostring(Profile.licenseType) contains 'SLES_SAP' or ImageOffer contains 'sap', 'SAP signal; ', '')))
| extend Workload = iff(isempty(Workload), 'Unclassified - guest/workload assessment required', Workload),
         AssociationEvidence = strcat('Compute managedBy: ', tostring(VM.managedBy), '; Managed owners: ', tostring(OwnerIds), '; AVD host pools: ', tostring(AvdHostPoolIds),
            '; SQL extensions/registrations: ', SqlExtensionRows, '/', SqlRegistrationRows, '; SAP extensions: ', SapExtensionRows),
         MigrationMethod = case(array_length(OwnerIds) > 1, 'Multiple owner associations - resolve ownership before changes',
            tostring(OwnerKinds) contains 'AKS', 'AKS node-pool workflow; validate surge, PDBs, persistence and capacity',
            tostring(OwnerKinds) contains 'Databricks', 'Databricks-supported node type/runtime and cluster restart/recreation',
            array_length(AvdHostPoolIds) > 0, 'AVD host replacement; check pooled/personal type, sessions, profiles and persistence',
            tostring(OwnerKinds) contains 'Batch', 'Batch pool replacement; drain tasks and review autoscale/quotas',
            array_length(OwnerKinds) > 0 or isnotempty(tostring(VM.managedBy)), 'Use owning service/vendor migration workflow; do not directly resize managed compute',
            SqlExtensionRows > 0 or SqlRegistrationRows > 0 or Workload contains 'SAP', 'Stateful/vendor assessment before resize or rebuild; validate HA, licensing, backup and rollback',
            'Determine application owner/state; then validate resize versus rebuild and rollback'),
         MigrationApproval = 'Not assessed - application, HA, backup and rollback require evidence'

| extend OsHintSource = iff(isnotempty(GuestOsName), 'Guest OS metadata', 'Image hint only - current guest unverified'),
         OsHintText = tolower(iff(isnotempty(GuestOsName), GuestOsName, strcat(ImagePublisher, ' ', ImageOffer, ' ', ImageSku)))
| extend NvmeOsHint = case(
        isempty(trim(' ', OsHintText)), 'Unknown - OS metadata missing',
        OsHintText matches regex @'windows.?server[^0-9]{0,30}(2019|2022|2025)($|[^0-9])', 'Listed OS family - validate image, generation and boot',
        OsHintText matches regex @'ubuntu[^0-9]{0,30}(18[._-]04|20[._-]04|22[._-]04|24[._-]04)($|[^0-9])', 'Listed OS family - validate image, kernel and boot',
        OsHintText matches regex @'debian[^0-9]{0,30}(11|12)($|[^0-9])', 'Listed OS family - validate image, kernel and boot',
        'Review official NVMe OS list - not classified by captured subset'),
         ManaOsHint = case(
        isempty(trim(' ', OsHintText)), 'Unknown - OS metadata missing',
        OsHintText matches regex @'windows.?server[^0-9]{0,30}(2016|2019|2022|2025)($|[^0-9])', 'Listed OS family - install/verify current MANA driver',
        OsHintText matches regex @'ubuntu[^0-9]{0,30}(22[._-]04|24[._-]04|26[._-]04)($|[^0-9])', 'Listed OS family - verify minimum kernel and MANA driver',
        'Review official MANA OS/kernel list - not classified by captured subset')
| extend NvmeDriverValidation = 'Unknown - guest driver and target boot not tested',
         ManaDriverValidation = 'Unknown - target hardware, driver and traffic not tested',
         NvmeOsReference = 'https://learn.microsoft.com/azure/virtual-machines/enable-nvme-interface',
         ManaOsReference = 'https://learn.microsoft.com/azure/virtual-network/accelerated-networking-overview',
         OsReferenceChecked = '2026-11-18'
| extend CatalogueRecord = tostring(parse_json('{ ...lifecycle lookup, see note below... }')[VmSize])
| extend CatalogueCoverage = iff(isempty(CatalogueRecord), 'Not assessed - SKU absent from captured catalogue', tostring(split(CatalogueRecord, '|')[0]))
| where '{DiscoveryWorkload}' == 'All' or Workload contains '{DiscoveryWorkload}'

| project Name, ResourceGroup, SubscriptionId, Region, VmSize, CatalogueCoverage, Workload, MigrationMethod,
    AcceleratedNetworking, ExpectedNics, DataDiskCount, SharedDiskEvidence, EphemeralOsDisk,
    UnmanagedDiskEvidence, Hibernation, SecurityType, SystemAssignedIdentity, MarketplacePlan, AdeEvidence,
    AdeDiskRows, ApplianceEvidence, PlacementEvidence, DiskVisibilityComplete, NicVisibilityComplete,
    ProvisionedDiskIops, ProvisionedDiskMBps,
    OsHintSource, NvmeOsHint, ManaOsHint, NvmeDriverValidation, ManaDriverValidation,
    OsReferenceChecked, NvmeOsReference, ManaOsReference, AssociationEvidence, OwnerIds, AvdHostPoolIds,
    SqlGroupIds, MigrationApproval, InventoryRole, ResourceId
| order by Workload asc, Name asc
```

### `discovery-pools`

Configured AKS/Batch pool sizes, autoscaler maxima, surge and node targets; not running-node counts or automatic additions to the Compute quota plan.

- **Visualization:** table
- **Scope:** `{Subscriptions}`
- **Base:** appended to the shared base query above

```kusto
resources
| union batchresources
| where type in~ ('microsoft.containerservice/managedclusters', 'microsoft.batch/batchaccounts/pools')
| extend Workload = iff(type =~ 'microsoft.containerservice/managedclusters', 'AKS', 'Batch')
| where '{DiscoveryWorkload}' == 'All' or Workload contains '{DiscoveryWorkload}'
| extend Pools = iff(Workload == 'AKS', properties.agentPoolProfiles, pack_array(properties))
| mv-expand Pool = Pools limit 2000
| extend VmSize = tostring(Pool.vmSize)
| extend CatalogueRecord =
